## 74. `GUIDEGVK/bin/api.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""GVK api v2: атомарные операции с репозиторием по правилам протокола.

Отличия v2 (все — исправления найденных дефектов, см. CHANGELOG v5.2):
  * журнал событий шардирован по сессиям: чужие строки не теряются (S5, R9);
  * дописывание в общий файл — с перечитыванием содержимого, а не только sha;
  * один GET даёт и sha, и содержимое (было три);
  * guard и валидатор вызываются до записи, а не «по совести» (S3, C12.4);
  * `get` читает каталоги и файлы >1 МБ, а не падает и не отдаёт пустоту;
  * 422 «уже существует» отличается от 422 «неверный запрос»;
  * время и дата — из заголовка Date GitHub (C4), не из часов песочницы;
  * `slice` — минимальный срез состояния за 1–3 вызова (C6, E1).

  api.py now
  api.py list   --repo O/R --path GVK/claims
  api.py get    --repo O/R --path GVK/STATE.md [--ref main]
  api.py slice  --repo O/R
  api.py claim  --repo O/R --role DEV --path GVK/claims/007.md --file /tmp/c.md
  api.py put    --repo O/R --role DEV --path REPORT/frag/007.json --file /tmp/007.json --schema report
  api.py append --repo O/R --role TZFIX --path GVK/STAGES.md --file /tmp/line.md
  api.py event  --repo O/R --role DEV --owner gvk-qwen-7 --file /tmp/event.json
  api.py delete --repo O/R --role DEV --path GVK/claims/007.md
  api.py merge  --repo O/R --role CLOSER --number 12

Коды возврата: 0 успех, 1 отказ, 2 ошибка, 3 занято. Токен не печатается никогда.
"""
import argparse, base64, json, os, random, subprocess, sys, time, urllib.error, urllib.request

API = "https://api.github.com"
TOKEN_FILE = "/opt/.t"
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
G = os.path.dirname(HERE)
GUARD = os.path.join(HERE, "guard.py")
VALIDATE = os.path.join(HERE, "validate.py")
SCHEMA_DIR = os.path.join(G, "schema")
TEMPLATE = os.path.join(G, "pep.template.json")


def token():
    t = os.environ.get("T") or (open(TOKEN_FILE).read().strip() if os.path.exists(TOKEN_FILE) else "")
    if not t:
        print("API ERROR: токен не найден"); sys.exit(2)
    return t


def call(method, url, body=None, tries=5, raw=False):
    """Один вызов с повторами по R2. Возвращает (status, json|text, headers)."""
    t = token()
    pause = 1.0
    for k in range(1, tries + 1):
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, method=method)
        req.add_header("Authorization", "Bearer " + t)
        req.add_header("Accept", "application/vnd.github.raw" if raw else "application/vnd.github+json")
        req.add_header("User-Agent", "gvk-protocol/5.2")
        data = None
        if body is not None:
            data = json.dumps(body).encode()
            req.add_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, data, timeout=45) as r:
                text = r.read().decode("utf-8", "replace")
                hdr = dict(r.headers)
                if raw:
                    return r.status, text, hdr
                try:
                    return r.status, json.loads(text), hdr
                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    return r.status, text, hdr
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
            text = e.read().decode("utf-8", "replace")
            hdr = dict(e.headers)
            temporary = e.code in (429, 500, 502, 503, 504)
            if e.code == 403 and (hdr.get("Retry-After") or hdr.get("X-RateLimit-Remaining") == "0"):
                temporary = True                      # вторичный лимит GitHub — временный отказ
            if temporary and k < tries:
                ra = hdr.get("Retry-After")
                wait = float(ra) if ra and ra.isdigit() else pause * (1 + random.uniform(-0.3, 0.3))
                print(f"API RETRY {k}/{tries}: {e.code}, пауза {min(wait, 60):.1f} c")
                time.sleep(min(wait, 60)); pause *= 2; continue
            try:
                return e.code, json.loads(text), hdr
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                return e.code, text, hdr
        except Exception as e:
            if k < tries:
                wait = pause * (1 + random.uniform(-0.3, 0.3))
                print(f"API RETRY {k}/{tries}: {type(e).__name__}, пауза {min(wait, 60):.1f} c")
                time.sleep(min(wait, 60)); pause *= 2; continue
            print(f"API ERROR: {type(e).__name__} после {tries} попыток"); sys.exit(2)
    print("API ERROR: попытки исчерпаны"); sys.exit(2)


def contents(repo, path, ref=None):
    return f"{API}/repos/{repo}/contents/{path}" + (f"?ref={ref}" if ref else "")


def gh_now():
    """Время GitHub в ISO-8601 UTC (C4). Часы песочницы источником не являются."""
    import email.utils, datetime
    st, _, hdr = call("HEAD", API + "/rate_limit")
    d = hdr.get("Date") or hdr.get("date") or ""
    try:
        dt = email.utils.parsedate_to_datetime(d).astimezone(datetime.timezone.utc)
        return dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"), dt.strftime("%Y%m%d"), dt.strftime("%H%M%S")
    except Exception:
        print("API ERROR: заголовок Date не получен, время неизвестно"); sys.exit(2)


def fetch(repo, path, ref=None):
    """Один вызов: (status, sha, text). Большие файлы дочитываются raw-запросом."""
    st, body, _ = call("GET", contents(repo, path, ref))
    if st != 200 or not isinstance(body, dict):
        return st, None, None
    if body.get("encoding") == "base64" and body.get("content"):
        return st, body.get("sha"), base64.b64decode(body["content"]).decode("utf-8", "replace")
    st2, raw, _ = call("GET", contents(repo, path, ref), raw=True)   # >1 МБ: content пуст
    return st, body.get("sha"), raw if st2 == 200 else None


def readback(repo, path, expect=None, ref=None):
    st, _, got = fetch(repo, path, ref)
    if st != 200 or got is None:
        print(f"READBACK FAIL: {path} не читается ({st})"); return False
    if expect is not None and got.strip() != expect.strip():
        print(f"READBACK FAIL: {path} содержимое не совпало"); return False
    print(f"READBACK OK: {path}"); return True


def guard_check(a, path, file=None, intent="write", extra=()):
    """S3: проверка прав кодом до действия. Отказ guard — отказ записи."""
    if a.no_guard:
        print("GUARD SKIPPED: --no-guard (только бутстрап реестра или рычаг владельца)")
        return True
    if not a.role:
        print("API ERROR: для записи нужен --role (S3) или явный --no-guard"); return False
    pep = a.pep if os.path.exists(a.pep) else TEMPLATE
    if pep == TEMPLATE:
        print("GUARD: реестр проекта отсутствует, права взяты из шаблона протокола (бутстрап)")
    cmd = [sys.executable, GUARD, "--role", a.role, "--pep", pep, "--intent", intent]
    if path:
        cmd += ["--paths", path]
    if file:
        cmd += ["--content-file", file]
    if a.claim:
        cmd += ["--claim", a.claim]
    if a.branch and a.branch != "main":
        cmd += ["--branch", a.branch]
    else:
        cmd += ["--branch", a.branch or "main"]
    if a.owner_lever:
        cmd += ["--owner-lever"]
    cmd += list(extra)
    r = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
    print(r.stdout.strip())
    if r.returncode != 0:
        print(f"GUARD DENY: действие не выполнять (код {r.returncode})")
        return False
    return True


def validate_check(a, file):
    if not a.schema:
        return True
    sch = a.schema if os.path.exists(a.schema) else os.path.join(SCHEMA_DIR, a.schema + ".schema.json")
    if not os.path.exists(sch):
        print(f"API ERROR: схема не найдена: {sch}"); return False
    r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, VALIDATE, "--schema", sch, "--file", file], capture_output=True, text=True)
    print(r.stdout.strip())
    return r.returncode == 0


def busy_or_bad(st, body):
    """422/409 при атомарном создании: занято или неверный запрос."""
    msg = (body.get("message", "") if isinstance(body, dict) else str(body)).lower()
    errors = json.dumps(body.get("errors", []), ensure_ascii=False).lower() if isinstance(body, dict) else ""
    if "already_exists" in errors or "already exists" in msg or st == 409:
        return 3
    if "sha" in errors and "wasn't supplied" in errors:
        return 3
    return 1


def put_payload(a, path, text, sha=None, message=None):
    payload = {"message": message or a.message, "branch": a.branch,
               "content": base64.b64encode(text.encode()).decode()}
    if sha:
        payload["sha"] = sha
    return payload


def do_write(a, path, text, atomic=False, schema=None, intent="write", message=None):
    if schema and not validate_check(a, a.file):
        print("API FAIL: контракт не сходится, запись запрещена (C12.4)"); return 1
    if not guard_check(a, path, a.file, intent):
        return 1
    sha = None
    if not atomic:
        st, sha, _ = fetch(a.repo, path, a.branch)
    payload = put_payload(a, path, text, sha, message)
    st, body, _ = call("PUT", contents(a.repo, path), payload)
    if st in (409, 422):
        code = busy_or_bad(st, body)
        if atomic:
            print(f"BUSY {st}: занято другим агентом" if code == 3 else f"API FAIL {st}: неверный запрос создания")
            return code
        st2, sha2, cur = fetch(a.repo, path, a.branch)      # одна повторная попытка (R7)
        if sha2:
            payload = put_payload(a, path, text, sha2, message)
            st, body, _ = call("PUT", contents(a.repo, path), payload)
    if st not in (200, 201):
        msg = body.get("message") if isinstance(body, dict) else str(body)[:120]
        print(f"API FAIL {st}: {msg}"); return 1
    print(f"WROTE {path} ({st})")
    return 0 if readback(a.repo, path, text, a.branch) else 1


def do_append(a, path, line, schema=None):
    """Дописывание в общий файл: при конфликте перечитывается СОДЕРЖИМОЕ, не только sha."""
    if schema and not validate_check(a, a.file):
        return 1
    if not guard_check(a, path, a.file, "update"):
        return 1
    for attempt in (1, 2, 3):
        st, sha, cur = fetch(a.repo, path, a.branch)
        base = cur or ""
        if line.strip() in base:
            print(f"APPEND SKIP: строка уже в {path}"); return 0
        text = base + ("" if not base or base.endswith("\n") else "\n") + line.rstrip() + "\n"
        payload = put_payload(a, path, text, sha)
        st, body, _ = call("PUT", contents(a.repo, path), payload)
        if st in (200, 201):
            st2, _, got = fetch(a.repo, path, a.branch)
            if got and line.strip() in got and all(l in got for l in base.splitlines() if l.strip()):
                print(f"APPENDED {path}: своё добавлено, чужое сохранено")
                return 0
            print("READBACK FAIL: после дописывания потеряны строки"); return 1
        if st not in (409, 422):
            msg = body.get("message") if isinstance(body, dict) else str(body)[:120]
            print(f"API FAIL {st}: {msg}"); return 1
        print(f"APPEND RETRY {attempt}: конфликт, перечитываю содержимое")
    print("API FAIL: три конфликта подряд, журнал не дописан"); return 1


def do_slice(a):
    """Минимальный срез C6 за 1–3 вызова: дерево + признаки плана."""
    st, tree, _ = call("GET", f"{API}/repos/{a.repo}/git/trees/{a.ref}?recursive=1")
    if st != 200 or not isinstance(tree, dict):
        print(f"API FAIL {st}: дерево не читается"); return 1
    paths = [t["path"] for t in tree.get("tree", []) if t.get("type") == "blob"]
    def under(p):
        return sorted(x for x in paths if x.startswith(p))
    claims = under("GVK/claims/")
    fixes = under("GVK/fixes/")
    roles = under("GVK/roles/")
    frag = under("REPORT/frag/")
    findings = under("REPORT/finding/")
    reviews = under("REPORT/review/")
    tasks = [x for x in under("REPORT/test/") if "/T-" in x]
    results = [x for x in under("REPORT/test/") if "/R-" in x]
    tclaims = under("REPORT/test/claims/")
    out = {
        "repo": a.repo, "ref": a.ref, "truncated": bool(tree.get("truncated")),
        "final": "GVK/FINAL.md" in paths, "stop": "GVK/STOP.md" in paths,
        "stopfix": "GVK/stopfix.md" in paths, "access": "GVK/ACCESS.md" in paths,
        "pep": "GVK/PEP.json" in paths, "stages": "GVK/STAGES.md" in paths,
        "limits": "GVK/LIMITS.md" in paths, "plan": "GVK/PLAN.md" in paths,
        "dict_project": "GVK/DICT.md" in paths,
        "claims": [os.path.basename(x) for x in claims],
        "fixes": [os.path.basename(x) for x in fixes],
        "roles": [os.path.basename(x) for x in roles],
        "frag_reports": [os.path.basename(x) for x in frag],
        "findings": [os.path.basename(x) for x in findings],
        "reviews": [os.path.basename(x) for x in reviews],
        "test_plan": "REPORT/test/PLAN.json" in paths,
        "test_tasks": [os.path.basename(x) for x in tasks],
        "test_results": [os.path.basename(x) for x in results],
        "test_claims": [os.path.basename(x) for x in tclaims],
        "compliance": "REPORT/COMPLIANCE.json" in paths,
        "stack_report": "REPORT/stack.json" in paths,
        "tokens_sessions": len(under("REPORT/tokens/")),
        "skeleton_present": [x for x in paths if os.path.basename(x) in
                             ("pom.xml", "pyproject.toml", "requirements.txt", "go.mod", "Cargo.toml", "package.json")
                             or x.endswith((".csproj", ".sln"))],
        "files_total": len(paths),
    }
    if a.with_state:
        for p in ("GVK/STAGES.md", "GVK/STOP.md", "GVK/LIMITS.md"):
            if p in paths:
                st2, _, txt = fetch(a.repo, p, a.ref)
                if txt:
                    out[p] = txt.strip().splitlines()[-6:]
    st3, br, _ = call("GET", f"{API}/repos/{a.repo}/branches/gvk-plan")
    out["plan_branch"] = st3 == 200
    sys.stdout.write(json.dumps(out, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1) + "\n")
    return 0


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("cmd", choices=["now", "list", "get", "slice", "claim", "put", "append", "event", "delete", "merge"])
    ap.add_argument("--repo"); ap.add_argument("--path"); ap.add_argument("--file")
    ap.add_argument("--message", default="gvk"); ap.add_argument("--ref", default="main")
    ap.add_argument("--branch", default="main"); ap.add_argument("--role"); ap.add_argument("--owner")
    ap.add_argument("--pep", default="GVK/PEP.json"); ap.add_argument("--claim"); ap.add_argument("--schema")
    ap.add_argument("--no-guard", action="store_true"); ap.add_argument("--owner-lever", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--legacy-events", action="store_true"); ap.add_argument("--with-state", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--number", type=int); ap.add_argument("--merge-fragment"); ap.add_argument("--json", action="store_true")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    if not a.repo:
        print("API ERROR: нужен --repo"); return 2

    if a.cmd == "now":
        iso, day, hms = gh_now()
        print(f"{iso} {day} {hms}")
        return 0

    if a.cmd == "slice":
        return do_slice(a)

    if a.cmd == "list":
        if not a.path:
            print("API ERROR: нужен --path"); return 2
        st, body, _ = call("GET", contents(a.repo, a.path, a.ref))
        if st != 200:
            print(f"API FAIL {st}: каталог не читается"); return 1
        if isinstance(body, dict):
            print(body.get("name", "")); return 0
        for it in body:
            print(it["name"] if not a.json else json.dumps(
                {"name": it["name"], "sha": it["sha"], "size": it.get("size")}, ensure_ascii=False))
        return 0

    if a.cmd == "get":
        if not a.path:
            print("API ERROR: нужен --path"); return 2
        st, body, _ = call("GET", contents(a.repo, a.path, a.ref))
        if st == 404:
            print("NOT FOUND"); return 1
        if st != 200:
            print(f"API FAIL {st}"); return 1
        if isinstance(body, list):
            for it in body:
                print(it["name"])
            return 0
        st2, _, txt = fetch(a.repo, a.path, a.ref)
        if txt is None:
            print("API FAIL: содержимое не прочитано"); return 1
        sys.stdout.write(txt)
        return 0

    if a.cmd == "merge":
        if not a.number:
            print("API ERROR: нужен --number (номер PR)"); return 2
        extra = ["--merge"]
        if a.merge_fragment:
            extra += ["--merge-fragment", a.merge_fragment]
        if not guard_check(a, None, None, "write", extra):
            return 1
        st, body, _ = call("PUT", f"{API}/repos/{a.repo}/pulls/{a.number}/merge", {"merge_method": "merge"})
        if st != 200:
            msg = body.get("message") if isinstance(body, dict) else str(body)[:120]
            print(f"API FAIL {st}: {msg}"); return 1
        print(f"MERGED PR {a.number}: {body.get('sha', '')[:7]}")
        return 0

    if not a.file or not os.path.exists(a.file):
        print("API ERROR: нужен существующий --file"); return 2
    text = open(a.file, encoding="utf-8").read()

    if a.cmd == "event":
        if not text.strip().startswith("{"):
            print("API ERROR: событие должно быть JSON"); return 2
        if not a.owner:
            print("API ERROR: нужен --owner (шард журнала принадлежит сессии)"); return 2
        iso, day, hms = gh_now()
        try:
            ev = json.loads(text)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
            print(f"API ERROR: событие не парсится: {e}"); return 2
        ev.setdefault("at", iso)
        ev.setdefault("owner", a.owner)
        if a.role:
            ev.setdefault("role", a.role)
        tmp = a.file + ".norm"
        open(tmp, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(json.dumps(ev, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n")
        a.file = tmp
        line = open(tmp, encoding="utf-8").read()
        if a.legacy_events:
            path = a.path or f"REPORT/events/{day}.jsonl"
            return do_append(a, path, line, schema="event")
        path = a.path or f"REPORT/events/{day}/{a.owner}-{a.role or 'ROLE'}-{hms}.jsonl"
        st, sha, cur = fetch(a.repo, path, a.branch)
        if st == 200 and cur is not None:
            return do_append(a, path, line, schema="event")     # свой шард: чужих строк нет
        return do_write(a, path, line, atomic=True, schema="event", intent="create")

    if a.cmd == "append":
        if not a.path:
            print("API ERROR: нужен --path"); return 2
        return do_append(a, a.path, text, schema=a.schema)

    if a.cmd == "claim":
        if not a.path:
            print("API ERROR: нужен --path"); return 2
        return do_write(a, a.path, text, atomic=True, schema=a.schema, intent="create")

    if a.cmd == "put":
        if not a.path:
            print("API ERROR: нужен --path"); return 2
        return do_write(a, a.path, text, schema=a.schema, intent="update")

    if a.cmd == "delete":
        if not a.path:
            print("API ERROR: нужен --path"); return 2
        if not guard_check(a, a.path, None, "delete"):
            return 1
        for attempt in (1, 2):
            st, sha, _ = fetch(a.repo, a.path, a.branch)
            if not sha:
                print("ALREADY GONE"); return 0
            st, body, _ = call("DELETE", contents(a.repo, a.path),
                               {"message": a.message, "sha": sha, "branch": a.branch})
            if st == 404:
                print("ALREADY GONE"); return 0
            if st == 409 and attempt == 1:
                print("DELETE RETRY: sha устарел, перечитываю")
                continue
            if st != 200:
                msg = body.get("message") if isinstance(body, dict) else str(body)[:120]
                print(f"API FAIL {st}: {msg}"); return 1
            st2, _, _ = fetch(a.repo, a.path, a.branch)
            print("DELETED" if st2 == 404 else "DELETE FAIL: файл всё ещё читается")
            return 0 if st2 == 404 else 1
    return 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        sys.exit(main())
    except SystemExit:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"API ERROR: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"); sys.exit(2)
```

## 75. `GUIDEGVK/bin/pep.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""GVK pep: реестр ролей без чтения реестра целиком (ECONOMY E2).

Реестр.machine полон, вход агента узок: агент печатает только свою карточку.
Полный `PEP.json` в контекст не грузить — это ~7k токенов вместо ~0.6k.

  pep.py card    --role DEV [--pep GVK/PEP.json]        # карточка роли + общий минимум
  pep.py init    --repo O/R --tz docs/TZ.md --stack python --kind code --out /tmp/PEP.json
  pep.py snap    --role DEV --owner gvk-qwen-7 --fragment 007 --state busy|free [--pep ...] --out ...
  pep.py check   [--pep GVK/PEP.json]                   # структура каталога и прав
  pep.py budget  [--pep ...] [--manifest ../MANIFEST.json]  # вход роли против limits.budget_input_tokens, код 1 при превышении
"""
import argparse, json, os, sys

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
G = os.path.dirname(HERE)
TEMPLATE = os.path.join(G, "pep.template.json")
SNAPSHOT = ("status", "owner", "fragment", "lease_until", "commit")
SLICE_TOKENS = 1200  # срез состояния C6, принятый расчётный вес (ECONOMY E1)


def est_text(text):
    cyr = sum(1 for c in text if "\u0400" <= c <= "\u04ff")
    return int(round(cyr / 4.4 + (len(text) - cyr) / 3.6))


def load(p):
    with open(p, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return json.load(f)


def dump(obj, out):
    txt = json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1) + "\n"
    if out:
        with open(out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(txt)
        print(f"PEP WROTE {out} ({len(txt.encode())} байт)")
    else:
        sys.stdout.write(txt)


def resolved_stack(pep):
    st = pep.get("stack", {}) or {}
    lang = (st.get("language") or "auto").lower()
    by = st.get("skeleton_by_stack", {}) or {}
    code = (st.get("code_by_stack", {}) or {}).get(lang, {}) or {}
    return {
        "language": lang,
        "version": st.get("version", ""),
        "packaging": st.get("packaging", ""),
        "skeleton": sorted(set(list(by.get("common", [])) + list(by.get(lang, [])) + list(st.get("skeleton", [])))),
        "app_code": sorted(set(list(code.get("app", [])) + list(st.get("app_code", [])))),
        "test_code": sorted(set(list(code.get("test", [])) + list(st.get("test_code", [])))),
        "code_roots": code.get("roots") or st.get("code_roots") or [],
        "version_file": (st.get("version_file", {}) or {}).get(lang, ""),
    }


def card_obj(pep, role):
    r = pep.get("roles", {}).get(role)
    if r is None:
        return None
    return {
        "role": role, "ver": r.get("ver"), "file": r.get("file"),
        "needs": r.get("needs", []), "lazy": r.get("lazy", []), "dicts": r.get("dicts", []),
        "singleton": r.get("singleton"), "claim": r.get("claim", ""),
        "lease_h": r.get("lease_h"), "lease_renew": r.get("lease_renew", False),
        "lever": r.get("lever"), "deficit": r.get("deficit", ""),
        "permissions": r.get("permissions", {}),
        "snapshot": {k: r.get(k) for k in SNAPSHOT if r.get(k) is not None},
        "common": {
            "pep_ver": pep.get("pep_ver"), "protocol_ver": pep.get("protocol_ver"),
            "repo": pep.get("repo"), "repo_kind": pep.get("repo_kind", "code"),
            "protocol_dir": pep.get("protocol_dir", ""),
            "stage": pep.get("stage"), "tz_path": pep.get("tz_path"), "tz_sha": pep.get("tz_sha"),
            "limits": pep.get("limits", {}), "priority": pep.get("priority", []),
            "hard_deny": pep.get("hard_deny", []),
            "stack": resolved_stack(pep),
        },
    }
def card(pep, role):
    out = card_obj(pep, role)
    if out is None:
        print(f"PEP ERROR: роль {role} отсутствует в каталоге"); return 2
    sys.stdout.write(json.dumps(out, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1) + "\n")
    return 0


def check(pep):
    errs = []
    for k in ("schema", "pep_ver", "protocol_ver", "repo", "stage", "tz_path", "priority", "roles"):
        if k not in pep:
            errs.append(f"нет поля {k}")
    if pep.get("repo_kind") not in ("code", "doc"):
        errs.append("repo_kind должен быть code или doc")
    for name in pep.get("priority", []):
        if name not in pep.get("roles", {}):
            errs.append(f"priority ссылается на отсутствующую роль {name}")
    for name, r in pep.get("roles", {}).items():
        for k in ("file", "needs", "singleton", "lease_h", "permissions", "ver", "claim"):
            if k not in r:
                errs.append(f"{name}: нет поля {k}")
        pm = r.get("permissions", {})
        for k in ("read", "write", "deny", "merge_main", "tools", "branches"):
            if k not in pm:
                errs.append(f"{name}.permissions: нет {k}")
        for k in ("write", "deny", "read", "branches", "create_only"):
            v = pm.get(k, [])
            if not isinstance(v, list) or any(not isinstance(x, str) for x in v):
                errs.append(f"{name}.permissions.{k} должен быть списком строк")
        if pm.get("merge_main") and name not in ("FIRST", "CLOSER"):
            errs.append(f"{name}: merge_main разрешён только FIRST и CLOSER (C10.6)")
        if r.get("singleton") and not r.get("claim") and name != "OBSERVER":
            errs.append(f"{name}: роль-одиночка без пути захвата")
        if name != "OBSERVER" and not r.get("singleton") and "{fragment}" not in r.get("claim", "") and "{task}" not in r.get("claim", ""):
            errs.append(f"{name}: масштабируемая роль без шаблона захвата")
    if errs:
        print("PEP CHECK FAIL:\n  " + "\n  ".join(errs[:30])); return 1
    print(f"PEP CHECK OK: {len(pep.get('roles', {}))} ролей, pep_ver {pep.get('pep_ver')}")
    return 0


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("cmd", choices=["card", "init", "snap", "check", "budget"])
    ap.add_argument("--pep", default="GVK/PEP.json")
    ap.add_argument("--role"); ap.add_argument("--owner"); ap.add_argument("--fragment")
    ap.add_argument("--state", choices=["busy", "free"], default="busy")
    ap.add_argument("--lease-until", default=""); ap.add_argument("--commit", default="")
    ap.add_argument("--repo"); ap.add_argument("--tz", default="docs/TZ.md"); ap.add_argument("--tz-sha", default="")
    ap.add_argument("--stack", default="auto"); ap.add_argument("--kind", choices=["code", "doc"], default="code")
    ap.add_argument("--at", default=""); ap.add_argument("--protocol-ver", default="")
    ap.add_argument("--protocol-dir", default="")
    ap.add_argument("--out"); ap.add_argument("--manifest")
    a = ap.parse_args()

    if a.cmd == "init":
        if not a.repo:
            print("PEP ERROR: нужен --repo"); return 2
        p = load(TEMPLATE)
        p.update({"repo": a.repo, "created_at": a.at, "created_by": a.owner or "", "tz_path": a.tz,
                  "tz_sha": a.tz_sha, "repo_kind": a.kind, "protocol_dir": a.protocol_dir})
        if a.protocol_ver:
            p["protocol_ver"] = a.protocol_ver
        p["stack"]["language"] = a.stack
        for r in p["roles"].values():
            r.update({"status": "свободен", "owner": None, "fragment": None, "lease_until": None, "commit": None})
        dump(p, a.out)
        return 0

    if not os.path.exists(a.pep):
        print(f"PEP ERROR: реестр не читается: {a.pep}"); return 2
    pep = load(a.pep)

    if a.cmd == "card":
        if not a.role:
            print("PEP ERROR: нужен --role"); return 2
        return card(pep, a.role)
    if a.cmd == "check":
        return check(pep)
    if a.cmd == "snap":
        if not a.role or a.role not in pep.get("roles", {}):
            print("PEP ERROR: нужен существующий --role"); return 2
        r = pep["roles"][a.role]
        if a.state == "busy":
            r.update({"status": "занят", "owner": a.owner or r.get("owner"),
                      "fragment": a.fragment, "lease_until": a.lease_until or None,
                      "commit": a.commit or None})
        else:
            r.update({"status": "свободен", "owner": None, "fragment": None, "lease_until": None, "commit": None})
        dump(pep, a.out)
        return 0
    if a.cmd == "budget":
        man = load(a.manifest or os.path.join(os.path.dirname(G), "MANIFEST.json"))
        tok = {f: v.get("tokens_est", 0) for f, v in man.get("files", {}).items()}
        limit = int((pep.get("limits", {}) or {}).get("budget_input_tokens", 0)) or 0
        rows, over = [], []
        for name, r in pep.get("roles", {}).items():
            files = ["PROTOCOL.md", "GUIDEGVK/COMMON.md", "GUIDEGVK/" + os.path.basename(r.get("file", ""))]
            files += ["GUIDEGVK/" + m for m in r.get("needs", [])]
            card_txt = json.dumps(card_obj(pep, name), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)
            base = sum(tok.get(f, 0) for f in files) + est_text(card_txt) + SLICE_TOKENS
            lazy = sum(tok.get("GUIDEGVK/" + m, 0) for m in r.get("lazy", []))
            rows.append((name, base, lazy))
            if limit and base > limit:
                over.append(f"{name} {base} > {limit}")
        print(f"срез состояния принят за {SLICE_TOKENS} токенов; предел входа {limit or 'не задан'}")
        print("роль | вход без lazy (оценка токенов) | все lazy худший случай")
        for name, base, lazy in sorted(rows, key=lambda x: -x[1]):
            mark = "  ПРЕВЫШЕНИЕ" if limit and base > limit else ""
            print(f"{name} | {base} | {base + lazy}{mark}")
        if over:
            print("PEP BUDGET FAIL: " + "; ".join(over)); return 1
        print("PEP BUDGET OK: все роли в пределах бюджета входа")
        return 0
    return 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        sys.exit(main())
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"PEP ERROR: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"); sys.exit(2)
```

## 76. `GUIDEGVK/bin/dict.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""GVK dict: трёхуровневые словари сжатия и измеримая выгода (ECONOMY E3–E6).

Уровни: GLOBAL (все роли) → GROUP (группа ролей) → ROLE (одна роль) → PROJECT
(домен проекта, `GVK/DICT.md`). Коды самоадресные: `%<адрес словаря><номер>`,
поэтому чужой сжатый текст читается без знания роли автора.

  dict.py load    --role DEV [--pep GVK/PEP.json]        # активный словарь роли (нужен редко)
  dict.py load    --role DEV --codes %g3,%d1             # раскрыть только встреченные коды
  dict.py encode  --role DEV --in /tmp/007.json --out /tmp/007c.json
  dict.py decode  --in /tmp/007c.json
  dict.py audit   --corpus REPORT --corpus GVK           # выгода по факту корпуса
  dict.py propose --corpus REPORT --ns j                 # кандидаты в словарь проекта
  dict.py lint    --in /tmp/007c.json                    # коды известны, зоны соблюдены
"""
import argparse, glob, json, os, re, sys

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
G = os.path.dirname(HERE)
DICT_DIR = os.path.join(G, "dict")
ZONES = {"remaining", "remarks", "symptom", "manual_notes", "why", "note", "detail", "proof", "evidence"}
FORBID_KEYS = {"schema", "schema_ver", "id", "fragment", "task", "status", "verdict", "severity",
               "event", "kind", "env", "level", "owner", "role", "branch", "at", "lease_until",
               "tz_sha", "last_green", "checked_sha", "base_sha", "fixed_sha", "sha", "path",
               "file", "line", "packages", "protocol_ver", "model", "finding_id", "change_id"}
NS = {"GLOBAL": "g", "GROUP-CODE": "k", "GROUP-TEST": "t", "GROUP-STATE": "s",
      "ROLE-FIRST": "f", "ROLE-DEV": "d", "ROLE-CLEANER": "c", "ROLE-REVIEWER": "r",
      "ROLE-PLANNER_TEST": "p", "ROLE-TESTER": "e", "ROLE-COMPLIANCE": "a", "ROLE-CLOSER": "l",
      "ROLE-TZFIX": "x", "ROLE-WISH": "w", "ROLE-OBSERVER": "o", "ROLE-TERMINATOR": "n",
      "ROLE-TERMINATOR2": "m", "ROLE-FIREFIGHTER": "i", "PROJECT": "j"}
CODE = re.compile(r"%[a-z][0-9a-z]{1,2}")
BLOCK = re.compile(r"<!--DICT:BEGIN-->(.*?)<!--DICT:END-->", re.S)

sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from tokens import est_text  # noqa: E402  один оценщик на весь протокол


def parse_dict(path):
    out = {}
    if not os.path.isfile(path):
        return out
    for line in open(path, encoding="utf-8"):
        s = line.rstrip("\n")
        if not s or s.startswith(("#", "---", "<!--", "|", "id:", "file:", "desc:", "name:", "etymology:", "F:", "Q:", "N:", "O:", "T:")):
            continue
        parts = s.split(" ", 1)
        if len(parts) == 2 and CODE.fullmatch(parts[0]) and parts[1].strip():
            out[parts[0]] = parts[1].strip()
    return out


def dict_paths(names, dict_dir=DICT_DIR, project=None):
    paths = []
    for n in names:
        if n == "PROJECT":
            if project:
                paths.append(project)
        else:
            paths.append(os.path.join(dict_dir, n + ".dict.md"))
    return paths


def active(role, pep_path=None, dict_dir=DICT_DIR, project=None, names=None):
    if names is None:
        names = ["GLOBAL"]
        # реестр проекта не найден — берём поставленный шаблон: групповые и ролевой
        # словари должны работать и без него, иначе роль молча теряет свои коды
        if pep_path and not os.path.isfile(pep_path):
            pep_path = os.path.join(G, "pep.template.json")
        if pep_path and os.path.isfile(pep_path):
            pep = json.load(open(pep_path, encoding="utf-8"))
            names = pep.get("roles", {}).get(role, {}).get("dicts", ["GLOBAL"])
            if project and os.path.isfile(project):
                names = list(names) + ["PROJECT"]
    merged, clashes, missing = {}, [], []
    for n in names:
        for p in dict_paths([n], dict_dir, project):
            if not os.path.isfile(p):
                missing.append(p); continue
            for code, phrase in parse_dict(p).items():
                if code in merged and merged[code] != phrase:
                    clashes.append(f"{code}: {os.path.basename(p)} против другого словаря")
                merged[code] = phrase
    return merged, clashes, missing


def all_dicts(dict_dir=DICT_DIR, project=None):
    merged = {}
    for p in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(dict_dir, "*.dict.md"))) + ([project] if project else []):
        merged.update(parse_dict(p))
    return merged


def encode_text(text, mapping):
    items = sorted(mapping.items(), key=lambda kv: -len(kv[1]))
    saved = 0
    for code, phrase in items:
        if phrase in text:
            n = text.count(phrase)
            saved += n * (est_text(phrase) - est_text(code))
            text = text.replace(phrase, code)
    return text, saved


def walk_json(obj, mapping, key=None):
    saved = 0
    if isinstance(obj, dict):
        out = {}
        for k, v in obj.items():
            out[k], s = walk_json(v, mapping, k)
            saved += s
        return out, saved
    if isinstance(obj, list):
        out = []
        for v in obj:
            r, s = walk_json(v, mapping, key)
            out.append(r); saved += s
        return out, saved
    if isinstance(obj, str) and key in ZONES and key not in FORBID_KEYS:
        return encode_text(obj, mapping)
    return obj, 0


def do_encode(a, mapping):
    raw = open(a.inp, encoding="utf-8").read()
    if a.inp.endswith(".json"):
        data = json.loads(raw)
        data, saved = walk_json(data, mapping)
        out = json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n"
    elif a.inp.endswith(".jsonl"):
        lines, saved = [], 0
        for ln in raw.splitlines():
            if not ln.strip():
                continue
            d, s = walk_json(json.loads(ln), mapping)
            saved += s
            lines.append(json.dumps(d, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")))
        out = "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
    else:
        saved = 0
        def repl(m):
            nonlocal saved
            t, s = encode_text(m.group(1), mapping)
            saved += s
            return "<!--DICT:BEGIN-->" + t + "<!--DICT:END-->"
        out = BLOCK.sub(repl, raw)
        if saved == 0:
            print("DICT: в md сжимать только внутри блока DICT:BEGIN/END — замен нет")
    if a.out:
        open(a.out, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(out)
    else:
        sys.stdout.write(out)
    print(f"DICT ENCODE: экономия ≈ {saved} токенов ({len(raw.encode())} → {len(out.encode())} байт)", file=sys.stderr)
    return 0


def do_decode(a, mapping):
    raw = open(a.inp, encoding="utf-8").read()
    inv = {c: p for c, p in mapping.items()}
    out = CODE.sub(lambda m: inv.get(m.group(0), m.group(0)), raw)
    if a.out:
        open(a.out, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(out)
    else:
        sys.stdout.write(out)
    return 0


def corpus_strings(dirs):
    vals = []
    for d in dirs:
        for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(d, "**", "*"), recursive=True):
            if not os.path.isfile(p):
                continue
            if p.endswith((".json", ".jsonl")):
                try:
                    txt = open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()
                    objs = [json.loads(l) for l in txt.splitlines() if l.strip()] if p.endswith(".jsonl") else [json.loads(txt)]
                except Exception:
                    continue
                def rec(o, key=None):
                    if isinstance(o, dict):
                        for k, v in o.items():
                            rec(v, k)
                    elif isinstance(o, list):
                        for v in o:
                            rec(v, key)
                    elif isinstance(o, str) and key in ZONES:
                        vals.append(o)
                for o in objs:
                    rec(o)
            elif p.endswith(".md"):
                for m in BLOCK.finditer(open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()):
                    vals.append(m.group(1))
    return vals


def do_audit(a, mapping):
    if not a.corpus:
        print("DICT ERROR: audit требует --corpus <каталог> (можно несколько): без корпуса выгода не считается, "
              "а совет «убрать» был бы ложным. Список записей — dict.py load")
        return 2
    vals = corpus_strings(a.corpus)
    if not vals:
        print("DICT ERROR: в корпусе нет служебных строк (REPORT/GVK пусты или не содержат нужных полей): "
              "выгоду посчитать нельзя, словарь не трогай")
        return 2
    rows, total = [], 0
    for code, phrase in sorted(mapping.items()):
        freq = sum(v.count(phrase) for v in vals)
        gain = freq * (est_text(phrase) - est_text(code))
        overhead = est_text(code + " " + phrase)
        total += gain
        rows.append((code, freq, gain, overhead, phrase))
    print("код | повторов в корпусе | выгода токенов | цена строки словаря | фраза")
    for code, freq, gain, overhead, phrase in rows:
        flag = "" if gain - overhead > 0 else "  ← невыгодно, убрать"
        print(f"{code} | {freq} | {gain} | {overhead} | {phrase[:60]}{flag}")
    print(f"ИТОГО выгода ≈ {total} токенов на корпус; записей {len(mapping)}")
    print("Словарь живёт в инструменте, а не в контексте (E4): цена загрузки в контекст не платится.")
    return 0


def do_propose(a, mapping):
    vals = corpus_strings(a.corpus or [])
    known = set(mapping.values())
    cand = {}
    for v in vals:
        for part in re.split(r"[;\n]|(?<=\.)\s", v):
            s = part.strip()
            if not s or s in known:
                continue
            if est_text(s) < a.min_tokens:
                continue
            cand[s] = cand.get(s, 0) + 1
    rows = [(s, n, n * (est_text(s) - 3)) for s, n in cand.items() if n >= a.min_freq]
    rows.sort(key=lambda r: -r[2])
    used = {c for c in mapping if c.startswith("%" + a.ns)}
    idx = 1
    print("# кандидаты в словарь проекта (проверить глазами, затем дописать в GVK/DICT.md)")
    for s, n, gain in rows[: a.limit]:
        while f"%{a.ns}{idx}" in used:
            idx += 1
        print(f"%{a.ns}{idx} {s}    # повторов {n}, выгода ≈ {gain}")
        idx += 1
    if not rows:
        print("# кандидатов нет: короткие или редкие фразы сжатие не выигрывают")
    return 0


def do_lint(a, mapping):
    raw = open(a.inp, encoding="utf-8").read()
    known = all_dicts(a.dict_dir, a.project)
    bad = []
    for m in CODE.finditer(raw):
        if m.group(0) not in known:
            bad.append(f"неизвестный код {m.group(0)}")
    if a.inp.endswith((".json", ".jsonl")):
        objs = [json.loads(l) for l in raw.splitlines() if l.strip()] if a.inp.endswith(".jsonl") else [json.loads(raw)]
        def rec(o, key=None):
            if isinstance(o, dict):
                for k, v in o.items():
                    if CODE.search(k):
                        bad.append(f"код в ключе JSON: {k}")
                    rec(v, k)
            elif isinstance(o, list):
                for v in o:
                    rec(v, key)
            elif isinstance(o, str) and CODE.search(o):
                if key not in ZONES:
                    bad.append(f"код в поле вне разрешённых зон: {key}")
        for o in objs:
            rec(o)
    if bad:
        print("DICT LINT FAIL:\n  " + "\n  ".join(sorted(set(bad))[:20])); return 1
    print("DICT LINT OK")
    return 0


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("cmd", choices=["load", "encode", "decode", "audit", "propose", "lint"])
    ap.add_argument("--role"); ap.add_argument("--pep", default="GVK/PEP.json")
    ap.add_argument("--dict-dir", default=DICT_DIR); ap.add_argument("--project")
    ap.add_argument("--names", nargs="*")
    ap.add_argument("--codes", help="через запятую: раскрыть только эти коды (дешёвый вход)")
    ap.add_argument("--in", dest="inp"); ap.add_argument("--out")
    ap.add_argument("--corpus", action="append")
    ap.add_argument("--ns", default="j"); ap.add_argument("--min-tokens", type=int, default=6)
    ap.add_argument("--min-freq", type=int, default=3); ap.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=20)
    a = ap.parse_args()

    if a.cmd == "load":
        if not a.role:
            print("DICT ERROR: нужен --role"); return 2
        m, clashes, missing = active(a.role, a.pep, a.dict_dir, a.project, a.names)
        if a.codes:
            want = [c.strip() for c in a.codes.split(",") if c.strip()]
            unknown = [c for c in want if c not in m]
            m = {c: v for c, v in m.items() if c in want}
            for c in unknown:
                print(f"# неизвестный код: {c}", file=sys.stderr)
        if clashes:
            print("DICT ERROR: коллизия кодов:\n  " + "\n  ".join(clashes)); return 2
        for p in missing:
            print(f"# словарь не поставлен: {p}", file=sys.stderr)
        for c, p in sorted(m.items()):
            print(f"{c} {p}")
        print(f"# записей {len(m)}, цена словаря ≈ {est_text(chr(10).join(f'{c} {p}' for c, p in m.items()))} токенов", file=sys.stderr)
        return 0
    if a.cmd in ("encode", "decode", "lint") and not a.inp:
        print("DICT ERROR: нужен --in"); return 2
    if a.cmd == "encode":
        m, clashes, _ = active(a.role or "", a.pep, a.dict_dir, a.project, a.names)
        if clashes:
            print("DICT ERROR: коллизия кодов"); return 2
        return do_encode(a, m)
    if a.cmd == "decode":
        return do_decode(a, all_dicts(a.dict_dir, a.project))
    if a.cmd == "lint":
        return do_lint(a, all_dicts(a.dict_dir, a.project))
    if a.cmd == "audit":
        return do_audit(a, all_dicts(a.dict_dir, a.project))
    return do_propose(a, all_dicts(a.dict_dir, a.project))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        sys.exit(main())
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"DICT ERROR: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"); sys.exit(2)
```

## 77. `GUIDEGVK/bin/tokens.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""GVK tokens: учёт расхода токенов сессии и сводка по проекту (ECONOMY E7–E10).

Честность важнее красоты: точные числа берутся только из счётчика платформы
(--exact-in/--exact-out). Всё остальное — оценка модели `gvk-bytes/1` и помечается
`source: estimate`. Выдавать оценку за точный расход запрещено (C10.19).

  tokens.py est     --files PROTOCOL.md GUIDEGVK/COMMON.md      # оценка входа
  tokens.py session --role DEV --owner gvk-qwen-7 --fragment 007 \
                    --read PROTOCOL.md GUIDEGVK/COMMON.md --wrote /tmp/007.json \
                    --calls 42 --model qwen3-coder --out /tmp/tokens.json
  tokens.py line    --file /tmp/tokens.json                     # строка для чата
  tokens.py rollup  --dir REPORT/tokens [--json]                # сводка проекта
"""
import argparse, json, os, sys, glob

CYR = (0x0400, 0x052F)
CYR_BPT = 4.4          # байт на токен для кириллицы (2 байта на знак, ~2.2 знака на токен)
OTH_BPT = 3.6          # байт на токен для латиницы, кода и пунктуации
ESTIMATOR = "gvk-bytes/1"


def est_text(text):
    cyr = oth = 0
    for ch in text:
        n = len(ch.encode("utf-8"))
        if CYR[0] <= ord(ch) <= CYR[1]:
            cyr += n
        else:
            oth += n
    return int(round(cyr / CYR_BPT + oth / OTH_BPT))


def est_files(paths):
    total, per = 0, {}
    for p in paths:
        if not os.path.isfile(p):
            per[p] = None
            continue
        t = est_text(open(p, encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").read())
        per[p] = t
        total += t
    return total, per


def read_text(p):
    return open(p, encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").read() if p and os.path.isfile(p) else ""


def session(a):
    reads = list(a.read or [])
    wrote = list(a.wrote or [])
    in_est, per_in = est_files(reads)
    in_est += est_text(read_text(a.chat_in))
    out_est, per_out = est_files(wrote)
    out_est += est_text(read_text(a.chat_out))
    exact_in = a.exact_in if a.exact_in is not None else None
    exact_out = a.exact_out if a.exact_out is not None else None
    ti = exact_in if exact_in is not None else in_est
    to = exact_out if exact_out is not None else out_est
    if exact_in is not None and exact_out is not None:
        source = "exact"
    elif exact_in is None and exact_out is None:
        source = "estimate"
    else:
        source = "mixed"
    rec = {
        "schema": "gvk/tokens/1", "schema_ver": "1",
        "at": a.at, "owner": a.owner, "role": a.role, "session_id": a.session_id or f"{a.owner}-{a.at}",
        "model": a.model or "unknown", "protocol_ver": a.protocol_ver or "",
        "source": source, "estimator": ESTIMATOR if source != "exact" else "",
        "in_tokens": ti, "out_tokens": to, "total_tokens": ti + to,
        "in_exact": exact_in, "out_exact": exact_out,
        "in_est": in_est, "out_est": out_est,
        "calls": a.calls or 0, "api_calls": a.api_calls or 0,
        "modules_read": reads, "artifacts_written": wrote,
        "bytes_read": sum(os.path.getsize(p) for p in reads if os.path.isfile(p)),
        "dict_saved_tokens": a.dict_saved or 0,
    }
    for k in ("fragment", "stage", "lever", "note", "tz_sha"):
        v = getattr(a, k, None)
        if v not in (None, ""):
            rec[k] = v
    txt = json.dumps(rec, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1) + "\n"
    if a.out:
        open(a.out, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(txt)
        print(f"TOKENS WROTE {a.out}: {rec['total_tokens']} ({source})")
    else:
        sys.stdout.write(txt)
    return 0


def line(a):
    d = json.load(open(a.file, encoding="utf-8"))
    n = d.get("total_tokens", 0)
    mark = "" if d.get("source") == "exact" else " (оценка)"
    print(f"расход токенов: {n}{mark}")
    return 0


def rollup(a):
    recs = []
    for p in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(a.dir, "**", "*.json"), recursive=True)):
        if os.path.basename(p) == "TOTAL.json":
            continue
        try:
            d = json.load(open(p, encoding="utf-8"))
        except Exception:
            continue
        if d.get("schema") == "gvk/tokens/1":
            recs.append(d)
    by = {}
    for d in recs:
        for key in ("role", "model", "fragment", "stage"):
            k = f"{key}:{d.get(key, '—')}"
            e = by.setdefault(k, {"sessions": 0, "total": 0, "exact": 0, "calls": 0})
            e["sessions"] += 1
            e["total"] += d.get("total_tokens", 0)
            e["exact"] += 1 if d.get("source") == "exact" else 0
            e["calls"] += d.get("calls", 0)
    total = sum(d.get("total_tokens", 0) for d in recs)
    exact = sum(1 for d in recs if d.get("source") == "exact")
    saved = sum(d.get("dict_saved_tokens", 0) for d in recs)
    out = {"schema": "gvk/tokens_total/1", "schema_ver": "1", "at": a.at or "",
           "sessions": len(recs), "total_tokens": total, "exact_sessions": exact,
           "estimated_sessions": len(recs) - exact, "dict_saved_tokens": saved,
           "by": dict(sorted(by.items()))}
    if a.json:
        sys.stdout.write(json.dumps(out, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1) + "\n")
        return 0
    print(f"сессий {len(recs)} | токенов {total} | точных {exact} | оценочных {len(recs) - exact} | словарная экономия {saved}")
    print("срез | сессий | токенов | точных | вызовов")
    for k, v in out["by"].items():
        if k.startswith("stage:") or k.startswith("fragment:"):
            continue
        print(f"{k} | {v['sessions']} | {v['total']} | {v['exact']} | {v['calls']}")
    return 0


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("cmd", choices=["est", "session", "line", "rollup"])
    ap.add_argument("--files", nargs="*"); ap.add_argument("--read", nargs="*"); ap.add_argument("--wrote", nargs="*")
    ap.add_argument("--chat-in"); ap.add_argument("--chat-out")
    ap.add_argument("--exact-in", type=int); ap.add_argument("--exact-out", type=int)
    ap.add_argument("--role"); ap.add_argument("--owner"); ap.add_argument("--fragment"); ap.add_argument("--stage", type=int)
    ap.add_argument("--model"); ap.add_argument("--protocol-ver"); ap.add_argument("--session-id")
    ap.add_argument("--lever"); ap.add_argument("--note"); ap.add_argument("--tz-sha")
    ap.add_argument("--calls", type=int); ap.add_argument("--api-calls", type=int); ap.add_argument("--dict-saved", type=int)
    ap.add_argument("--at", default=""); ap.add_argument("--out"); ap.add_argument("--file"); ap.add_argument("--dir", default="REPORT/tokens")
    ap.add_argument("--json", action="store_true")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    if a.cmd == "est":
        if not a.files:
            print("TOKENS ERROR: нужен --files"); return 2
        total, per = est_files(a.files)
        for p, t in per.items():
            print(f"{t if t is not None else 'НЕТ ФАЙЛА'}\t{p}")
        print(f"ИТОГО (оценка {ESTIMATOR}): {total}")
        return 0
    if a.cmd == "session":
        if not a.role or not a.owner:
            print("TOKENS ERROR: нужны --role и --owner"); return 2
        return session(a)
    if a.cmd == "line":
        if not a.file:
            print("TOKENS ERROR: нужен --file"); return 2
        return line(a)
    return rollup(a)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        sys.exit(main())
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"TOKENS ERROR: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"); sys.exit(2)
```

## 78. `GUIDEGVK/bin/doctor.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""GVK doctor: статический аудит поставки протокола. Владелец запускает после правки.

Ловит класс дефектов, который не виден в золотом случае: маршрут на несуществующий
модуль, роль, которой нечем писать её же артефакты, каркас стека вне прав FIRST,
схема с ключом, который валидатор не понимает, словарь с коллизией кодов,
рычаг без роли-исполнителя, битая ссылка на модуль.

  python3 bin/doctor.py [--root .] [--verbose]
Код возврата: 0 — чисто, 1 — есть замечания, 2 — ошибка аудита.
"""
import argparse, glob, json, os, re, subprocess, sys

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
RES = []

# Что каждая роль обязана уметь записывать по своим инструкциям (FM2, CT3, файлы ролей).
MUST_WRITE = {
    "FIRST": ["GVK/PLAN.md", "GVK/PEP.json", "GVK/AUDIT.md", "GVK/STAGES.md", "GVK/LIMITS.md",
              "GVK/PORTS.md", "GVK/LINKS.md", "GVK/claims/000.md", "GVK/roles/FIRST.md",
              "REPORT/frag/000.json", "REPORT/stack.json", "REPORT/tokens/x.json", "README.md"],
    "DEV": ["GVK/claims/007.md", "GVK/STATE.md", "GVK/REQUESTS.md", "GVK/AUDIT.md",
            "REPORT/frag/007.json", "REPORT/ckpt/007.json", "REPORT/events/20261122/a-DEV-1.jsonl",
            "REPORT/tokens/20261122/a-DEV-1.json", "README.md", "THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md", "GVK/STOP.md"],
    "CLEANER": ["GVK/fixes/007.md", "REPORT/frag/007.json", "REPORT/finding/007-ab12cd34.json",
                "REPORT/ckpt/007.json", "REPORT/tokens/x.json", "THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md", "GVK/STOP.md"],
    "REVIEWER": ["GVK/roles/REVIEWER.md", "REPORT/review/007-ab12cd34.json",
                 "REPORT/finding/007-ab12cd34.json", "REPORT/tokens/x.json", "GVK/STOP.md"],
    "PLANNER_TEST": ["GVK/roles/PLANNER_TEST.md", "REPORT/test/PLAN.json", "REPORT/test/T-001.json",
                     "REPORT/tokens/x.json"],
    "TESTER": ["REPORT/test/claims/T-001.json", "REPORT/test/R-001.json", "tests/test_x.py",
               "src/test/java/XTest.java", "REPORT/finding/007-ab12cd34.json", "REPORT/tokens/x.json"],
    "COMPLIANCE": ["GVK/roles/COMPLIANCE.md", "REPORT/COMPLIANCE.json", "THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md",
                   "licenses/MIT.txt", "REPORT/tokens/x.json"],
    "CLOSER": ["GVK/FINAL.md", "REPORT/FINAL.json", "CHANGELOG.md", "README.md", "README_RU.md",
               "pom.xml", "pyproject.toml", "GVK/claims/007.md", "REPORT/tokens/x.json"],
    "TZFIX": ["GVK/stopfix.md", "GVK/STOP.md", "GVK/STAGES.md", "GVK/CHANGE-001.md", "GVK/PLAN.md",
              "docs/TZ.md", "REPORT/tokens/x.json"],
    "WISH": ["GVK/roles/WISH.md", "GVK/PLAN.md", "GVK/PORTS.md", "GVK/CHANGE-001.md", "docs/TZ.md",
             "REPORT/tokens/x.json"],
    "OBSERVER": ["REPORT/tokens/20261122/o-OBSERVER-1.json", "REPORT/events/20261122/o-OBSERVER-1.jsonl"],
    "TERMINATOR": ["GVK/roles/TERMINATOR.md", "GVK/STOP.md", "GVK/STAGES.md", "GVK/PLAN.md",
                   "GVK/claims/007.md", "GVK/releases/x.md", "docs/TZ.md", "REPORT/tokens/x.json"],
    "TERMINATOR2": ["GVK/roles/TERMINATOR2.md", "GVK/claims/007.md", "GVK/fixes/007.md",
                    "GVK/releases/x.md", "GVK/LIMITS.md", "GVK/STOP.md", "REPORT/tokens/x.json"],
    "FIREFIGHTER": ["GVK/PLAN.md", "GVK/AUDIT.md", "src/app/x.py", "REPORT/tokens/x.json"],
}
# Рычаги, которые меняют состояние вне обычных прав роли: проверяются отдельно.
LEVER_ONLY = {"TERMINATOR2": {"GVK/LIMITS.md", "GVK/ACCESS.md"}}


def ck(name, ok, detail=""):
    RES.append((name, bool(ok), detail))


def load_guard():
    import importlib.util
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("gvkguard", os.path.join(HERE, "guard.py"))
    m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(m)
    return m


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--root", default=os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(HERE)))
    ap.add_argument("--verbose", action="store_true")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    root = os.path.abspath(a.root)
    G = os.path.join(root, "GUIDEGVK")
    pep = json.load(open(os.path.join(G, "pep.template.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
    guard = load_guard()
    sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
    from tokens import est_text

    # 1. манифест
    r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, os.path.join(HERE, "manifest.py"), "--root", root, "--check"],
                       capture_output=True, text=True)
    ck("манифест целостен", r.returncode == 0, r.stdout.strip()[:120])

    # 2. шапки md+
    bad_head, ids = [], {}
    for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(G, "**", "*.md"), recursive=True) + [os.path.join(root, "PROTOCOL.md")]:
        t = open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()
        name = os.path.basename(p)
        if not t.startswith("---\n"):
            bad_head.append(f"{name}: нет шапки"); continue
        head = t[4:t.index("\n---", 4)]
        f = dict(re.findall(r"^([a-zA-Z]+): (.*)$", head, re.M))
        for k in ("id", "file", "etymology", "desc", "name", "F", "Q"):
            if k not in f:
                bad_head.append(f"{name}: нет поля {k}")
        if f.get("file") != name:
            bad_head.append(f"{name}: file={f.get('file')}")
        if len(f.get("id", "")) > 17:
            bad_head.append(f"{name}: id длиннее 17")
        ids.setdefault(f.get("id"), []).append(name)
    ck("шапки md+ валидны", not bad_head, "; ".join(bad_head[:6]))
    dup = {k: v for k, v in ids.items() if len(v) > 1}
    ck("идентификаторы модулей уникальны", not dup, str(dup))

    # 3. ограждения
    fences, nested = [], []
    for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(root, "**", "*.md"), recursive=True):
        if os.path.basename(p).startswith("GVK_v5"):
            continue
        inside = False
        for ln in open(p, encoding="utf-8"):
            if re.match(r"^\s*`{3,}", ln):
                if inside and len(ln) - len(ln.lstrip("`")) > 3:
                    nested.append(os.path.basename(p))
                inside = not inside
        if inside:
            fences.append(os.path.basename(p))
    ck("тройные ограждения парные", not fences, ", ".join(fences))
    ck("вложенных ограждений нет", not nested, ", ".join(nested))

    # 4. маршрутизация
    miss = []
    for name, r in pep["roles"].items():
        for m in r.get("needs", []) + r.get("lazy", []):
            if not os.path.isfile(os.path.join(G, m)):
                miss.append(f"{name}: {m}")
        if not os.path.isfile(os.path.join(root, r["file"])):
            miss.append(f"{name}: файл роли {r['file']}")
        for d in r.get("dicts", []):
            if not os.path.isfile(os.path.join(G, "dict", d + ".dict.md")):
                miss.append(f"{name}: словарь {d}")
    ck("маршруты roles → модули и словари существуют", not miss, "; ".join(miss[:6]))
    ck("priority ссылается на существующие роли", all(x in pep["roles"] for x in pep["priority"]))
    lm = pep.get("lever_map", {})
    ck("lever_map ссылается на существующие роли", bool(lm) and all(v in pep["roles"] for v in lm.values()))
    lev = open(os.path.join(G, "LEVERS.md"), encoding="utf-8").read()
    table = lev[lev.index("| Команда | Роль |"):lev.index("L3. `продолжай`")]
    rows = [l for l in table.splitlines() if l.startswith("|")][2:]
    no_role = []
    for row in rows:
        cells = [c.strip() for c in re.split(r"(?<!\\)\|", row.strip("|"))]
        if len(cells) < 2:
            continue
        cmd, role = cells[0], cells[1]
        key = cmd.split(" ")[0].replace("\\", "")
        if role not in pep["roles"]:
            no_role.append(f"{cmd}: роль {role}")
        elif not any(k.startswith(key) or key.startswith(k.split(" ")[0]) for k in lm):
            no_role.append(f"{cmd}: нет в lever_map")
    ck("каждый рычаг таблицы имеет роль и запись в lever_map", not no_role, "; ".join(no_role[:6]))

    # 5. права покрывают обязанности роли
    tz = pep.get("tz_path", "docs/TZ.md")
    gaps = []
    for role, paths in MUST_WRITE.items():
        pm = pep["roles"][role]["permissions"]
        skel, app, test, roots = guard.stack_lists(pep)
        deny = [x for x in pm.get("deny", []) + pep.get("hard_deny", []) if not x.startswith("@")]
        if "@skeleton" in pm.get("deny", []):
            deny += skel
        if "@protocol" in pm.get("deny", []) and pep.get("protocol_dir"):
            deny += [pep["protocol_dir"].rstrip("/") + "/**"]
        allow = [tz if x == "@tz_path" else x for x in pm.get("write", [])]
        create = [tz if x == "@tz_path" else x for x in pm.get("create_only", [])]
        for p in paths:
            if p in LEVER_ONLY.get(role, set()):
                continue
            if guard.match(p, deny) and not guard.match(p, create):
                gaps.append(f"{role}: {p} в deny")
            elif not guard.match(p, allow + create):
                gaps.append(f"{role}: {p} вне write")
    ck("права ролей покрывают их артефакты", not gaps, "; ".join(gaps[:8]))

    # 6. каркас каждого стека доступен FIRST и закрыт DEV
    skel_gaps = []
    for lang, files in pep["stack"]["skeleton_by_stack"].items():
        if lang == "common":
            continue
        p2 = json.loads(json.dumps(pep)); p2["stack"]["language"] = lang
        skel, _, _, _ = guard.stack_lists(p2)
        first = pep["roles"]["FIRST"]["permissions"]
        for f in skel:
            probe = f.replace("**", "x").replace("*", "x")
            if not guard.match(probe, first["write"]):
                skel_gaps.append(f"FIRST не пишет {f} ({lang})")
        dev = pep["roles"]["DEV"]["permissions"]
        if not guard.match(probe, [x for x in dev["deny"] if not x.startswith("@")] + skel):
            skel_gaps.append(f"DEV может писать каркас {lang}")
    ck("каркас любого стека: FIRST пишет, DEV закрыт", not skel_gaps, "; ".join(sorted(set(skel_gaps))[:8]))

    # 7. схемы и валидатор
    sch_bad = []
    for p in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(G, "schema", "*.json"))):
        r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, os.path.join(HERE, "validate.py"), "--schema", p,
                            "--file", p, "--strict-keywords"], capture_output=True, text=True)
        if "неподдерживаемые ключи" in r.stdout:
            sch_bad.append(os.path.basename(p) + ": " + r.stdout.split("неподдерживаемые ключи:")[-1].strip()[:60])
    ck("схемы не используют непонятных валидатору ключей", not sch_bad, "; ".join(sch_bad[:4]))
    ct = open(os.path.join(G, "CONTRACTS.md"), encoding="utf-8").read()
    ex_bad = []
    for i, block in enumerate(re.findall(r"```json\n(.*?)\n```", ct, re.S)):
        try:
            obj = json.loads(block)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
            ex_bad.append(f"пример {i}: {e}"); continue
        key = (obj.get("schema") or "").split("/")[-2] if obj.get("schema") else ""
        sch = os.path.join(G, "schema", key + ".schema.json")
        if not key or not os.path.isfile(sch):
            ex_bad.append(f"пример {i}: нет схемы {key}"); continue
        f = os.path.join(root, f".doctor-ex{i}.json")
        open(f, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(block)
        r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, os.path.join(HERE, "validate.py"), "--schema", sch, "--file", f],
                           capture_output=True, text=True)
        os.remove(f)
        if r.returncode != 0:
            ex_bad.append(f"пример {i}: {r.stdout.strip()[:70]}")
    ck("примеры контрактов проходят свои схемы", not ex_bad, "; ".join(ex_bad[:4]))

    # 8. словари
    codes, dupc, weak = {}, [], []
    for p in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(G, "dict", "*.dict.md"))):
        for ln in open(p, encoding="utf-8"):
            if not ln.startswith("%"):
                continue
            code, _, phrase = ln.rstrip("\n").partition(" ")
            if code in codes:
                dupc.append(code)
            codes[code] = phrase
            if est_text(phrase) - est_text(code) < 5:
                weak.append(code)
    ck("коды словарей уникальны", not dupc, ", ".join(dupc))
    ck("записи словарей выгодны по длине", not weak, ", ".join(weak))
    dz = set(json.loads(json.dumps(pep.get("dicts", {}))).get("zones", []))
    import importlib.util
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("gvkdict", os.path.join(HERE, "dict.py"))
    dm = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); spec.loader.exec_module(dm)
    ck("зоны сжатия в реестре и в инструменте совпадают", dz == set(dm.ZONES), str(dz ^ set(dm.ZONES)))

    # 9. ссылки на модули
    names = {os.path.basename(p) for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(G, "**", "*.md"), recursive=True)}
    names |= {"PROTOCOL.md", "GUIDE.md", "CHANGELOG.md", "SELFTEST.md", "MANIFEST.json", "README.md",
              "README_RU.md", "THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md", "TZ.md", "PLAN.md", "STATE.md", "STOP.md",
              "STAGES.md", "LIMITS.md", "ACCESS.md", "AUDIT.md", "PORTS.md", "LINKS.md", "REQUESTS.md",
              "FINAL.md", "DICT.md", "PEP.md", "SKEL_JAVA.md", "SKEL_PY.md", "SKEL_OTHER.md",
              "DESIGN.md", "TODO.md", "SPEC.md", "NOTES.md", "LICENSES.md", "LICENSE",
              "CLAIM.md", "RELEASE.md", "TZFIX.md", "WISH.md"}
    broken = []
    for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(G, "*.md")) + [os.path.join(root, "PROTOCOL.md")]:
        for ref in re.findall(r"`([A-Z][A-Z_0-9]*\.md)`", open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()):
            if ref not in names and not ref.startswith(("CHANGE-", "ROLE_")):
                broken.append(f"{os.path.basename(p)} → {ref}")
    ck("ссылки на модули разрешаются", not broken, "; ".join(sorted(set(broken))[:6]))

    # 10. оценка токенов в манифесте совпадает с оценщиком
    man = json.load(open(os.path.join(root, "MANIFEST.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
    drift = []
    for f, v in man["files"].items():
        if not f.endswith(".md"):
            continue
        p = os.path.join(root, f)
        if os.path.isfile(p) and abs(est_text(open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()) - v.get("tokens_est", 0)) > 2:
            drift.append(f)
    ck("оценки токенов в манифесте согласованы с оценщиком", not drift, ", ".join(drift[:5]))

    # 19. строка «Нужны модули» в файле роли совпадает с needs реестра
    pep_t = json.load(open(os.path.join(G, "pep.template.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
    mismatch = []
    for name, r in pep_t.get("roles", {}).items():
        rp = os.path.join(G, os.path.basename(r.get("file", "")))
        if not os.path.isfile(rp):
            mismatch.append(f"{name}: нет файла роли"); continue
        txt = open(rp, encoding="utf-8").read()
        m = re.search(r"Нужны модули:(.*?)(?:\.\s|Грузить)", txt, re.S)
        stated = set(re.findall(r"[A-Z_]+\.md", m.group(1))) if m else set()
        if stated != set(r.get("needs", [])):
            mismatch.append(f"{name}: строка {sorted(stated)} против needs {sorted(r.get('needs', []))}")
    ck("строка «Нужны модули» совпадает с needs реестра", not mismatch, "; ".join(mismatch[:3]))

    # 20. бюджет входа: pep.py budget укладывается в limits.budget_input_tokens
    limit = int((pep_t.get("limits", {}) or {}).get("budget_input_tokens", 0))
    tok = {f: v.get("tokens_est", 0) for f, v in man["files"].items()}
    over = []
    for name, r in pep_t.get("roles", {}).items():
        files = ["PROTOCOL.md", "GUIDEGVK/COMMON.md", "GUIDEGVK/" + os.path.basename(r.get("file", ""))]
        files += ["GUIDEGVK/" + m for m in r.get("needs", [])]
        base = sum(tok.get(f, 0) for f in files)
        if limit and base > limit:
            over.append(f"{name} {base}>{limit}")
    ck("вход ролей укладывается в бюджет G1", bool(limit) and not over, "; ".join(over[:3]) or "limits.budget_input_tokens не задан")

    # 21. нет битых и чужеродных символов в тексте поставки
    junk = re.compile("[\ufffd\u3000-\u303f\u3040-\u30ff\u4e00-\u9fff\uac00-\ud7af]")
    hits = []
    for p in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(root, "**", "*"), recursive=True)):
        if not os.path.isfile(p) or os.path.basename(p).startswith("GVK_v5"):
            continue
        if not p.endswith((".md", ".py", ".json")):
            continue
        for n, line in enumerate(open(p, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"), 1):
            m = junk.search(line)
            if m:
                hits.append(f"{os.path.relpath(p, root)}:{n} {m.group()!r}")
    ck("нет битых и чужеродных символов", not hits, "; ".join(hits[:4]))

    # 22. процедура входа выполнима каждой ролью (рождение, захват, событие, учёт, снимок)
    entry_bad = []
    gp = os.path.join(HERE, "guard.py")
    pp = os.path.join(G, "pep.template.json")
    for role, r in pep["roles"].items():
        brs = r["permissions"].get("branches") or []
        br = (brs[0] if brs else "gvk/001").replace("{fragment}", "001").replace("<ROLE>", role)
        if "*" in br:
            br = "gvk/001"
        claim = (r.get("claim") or "").replace("{fragment}", "001").replace("{task}", "T-001")
        creates = [f"REPORT/events/20261122/a1-{role}-120000.jsonl",
                   f"REPORT/tokens/20261122/a1-{role}-120000.json"]
        if role != "OBSERVER":
            creates = ["GVK/births/20261122T1200-a1.md"] + creates + ([claim] if claim else [])
        groups = [("create", creates)]
        if role != "OBSERVER":
            groups.append(("update", ["GVK/PEP.json"]))
            if claim:
                groups.append(("delete", [claim]))
        for intent, paths in groups:
            if not paths:
                continue
            res = subprocess.run([sys.executable, gp, "--pep", pp, "--role", role, "--branch", br,
                                  "--intent", intent, "--paths"] + paths, capture_output=True, text=True)
            if res.returncode != 0:
                entry_bad.append(f"{role}/{intent}: {res.stdout.strip()[:90]}")
    ck("процедура входа выполнима каждой ролью", not entry_bad, "; ".join(entry_bad[:3]))

    ok = sum(1 for _, o, _ in RES if o)
    print(f"\nАУДИТ ПОСТАВКИ GVK — {ok}/{len(RES)} проверок\n")
    for n, o, d in RES:
        if o and not a.verbose:
            print(f"  OK   {n}")
        else:
            print(f"  {'OK  ' if o else 'ЗАМЕЧАНИЕ'} {n}" + (f" — {d}" if d else ""))
    return 0 if ok == len(RES) else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        sys.exit(main())
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"DOCTOR ERROR: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"); sys.exit(2)
```
